- **eagle-security-audit**: Deep security-focused code review. Hunts for vulnerabilities, injection vectors, auth flaws, data exposure, and supply chain risks. - **eagle-sentinel**: Use PROACTIVELY to verify finished work against acceptance criteria before it's called done — code diffs, generated docs/artifacts, contract changes, root-cause claims. - **eagle-spectral-investigate**: Root-cause debugging agent. Applies fix when root cause is confirmed. Use only for concrete bugs, failing tests, broken behavior, or root-cause investigations. Do not use for generic Spectral review requests; use eagle-spectral-triage for routing and the specialist review agents for review. - **eagle-spectral-plan**: Planning and architecture design agent. Do not use for generic Spectral review requests; use eagle-spectral-triage for routing and the specialist review agents for review. - **eagle-spectral-ship**: Pre-ship pipeline and PR creation agent. Do not use for generic Spectral review requests; use eagle-spectral-triage for routing and the specialist review agents for review. - **eagle-spectral-triage**: Routes broad Spectral review requests to the right Eagle review specialists. Read-only; reports, never fixes. Use after any change to a limiter, counter, idempotency, or cross-request-state surface. - **ecl-advisor-researcher**: Researches a single gray area decision and returns a structured comparison table with rationale. Spawned by discuss-phase advisor mode. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-advisor-researcher.md))
- **ecl-ai-researcher**: Researches a chosen AI framework's official docs to produce implementation-ready guidance — best practices, syntax, core patterns, and pitfalls distilled for the specific use case. Writes the Framework Quick Reference and Implementation Guidance sections of AI-SPEC.md. Spawned by /ecl:ai-integration-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-ai-researcher.md))
- **ecl-assumptions-analyzer**: Deeply analyzes codebase for a phase and returns structured assumptions with evidence. Spawned by discuss-phase assumptions mode. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-assumptions-analyzer.md))
- **ecl-code-fixer**: Applies fixes to code review findings from REVIEW.md. Reads source files, applies intelligent fixes, and commits each fix atomically. Spawned by /ecl:code-review --fix. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-code-fixer.md))
- **ecl-code-reviewer**: Reviews source files for bugs, security issues, and code quality problems. Produces structured REVIEW.md with severity-classified findings. Spawned by /ecl:code-review. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-code-reviewer.md))
- **ecl-codebase-mapper**: Explores codebase and writes structured analysis documents. Spawned by map-codebase with a focus area (tech, arch, quality, concerns). Writes documents directly to reduce orchestrator context load. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-codebase-mapper.md))
- **ecl-debug-session-manager**: Manages multi-cycle /ecl:debug checkpoint and continuation loop in isolated context. Spawns ecl-debugger agents, handles checkpoints via AskUserQuestion, dispatches specialist skills, applies fixes. Returns compact summary to main context. Spawned by /ecl:debug command. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-debug-session-manager.md))
- **ecl-debugger**: Investigates bugs using scientific method, manages debug sessions, handles checkpoints. Spawned by /ecl:debug orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-debugger.md))
- **ecl-doc-classifier**: Classifies a single planning document as ADR, PRD, SPEC, DOC, or UNKNOWN. Extracts title, scope summary, and cross-references. Spawned in parallel by /ecl:ingest-docs. Writes a JSON classification file and returns a one-line confirmation. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-doc-classifier.md))
- **ecl-doc-synthesizer**: Synthesizes classified planning docs into a single consolidated context. Applies precedence rules, detects cross-ref cycles, enforces LOCKED-vs-LOCKED hard-blocks, and writes INGEST-CONFLICTS.md with three buckets (auto-resolved, competing-variants, unresolved-blockers). Spawned by /ecl:ingest-docs. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-doc-synthesizer.md))
- **ecl-doc-verifier**: Verifies factual claims in generated docs against the live codebase. Returns structured JSON per doc. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-doc-verifier.md))
- **ecl-doc-writer**: Writes and updates project documentation. Spawned with a doc_assignment block specifying doc type, mode (create/update/supplement), and project context. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-doc-writer.md))
- **ecl-domain-researcher**: Researches the business domain and real-world application context of the AI system being built. Surfaces domain expert evaluation criteria, industry-specific failure modes, regulatory context, and what "good" looks like for practitioners in this field — before the eval-planner turns it into measurable rubrics. Spawned by /ecl:ai-integration-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-domain-researcher.md))
- **ecl-eval-auditor**: Retroactive audit of an implemented AI phase's evaluation coverage. Checks implementation against the AI-SPEC.md evaluation plan. Scores each eval dimension as COVERED/PARTIAL/MISSING. Produces a scored EVAL-REVIEW.md with findings, gaps, and remediation guidance. Spawned by /ecl:eval-review orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-eval-auditor.md))
- **ecl-eval-planner**: Designs a structured evaluation strategy for an AI phase. Identifies critical failure modes, selects eval dimensions with rubrics, recommends tooling, and specifies the reference dataset. Writes the Evaluation Strategy, Guardrails, and Production Monitoring sections of AI-SPEC.md. Spawned by /ecl:ai-integration-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-eval-planner.md))
- **ecl-executor**: Executes eCL plans with atomic commits, deviation handling, checkpoint protocols, and state management. Spawned by execute-phase orchestrator or execute-plan command. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-executor.md))
- **ecl-framework-selector**: Presents an interactive decision matrix to surface the right AI/LLM framework for the user's specific use case. Produces a scored recommendation with rationale. Spawned by /ecl:ai-integration-phase and /ecl-select-framework orchestrators. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-framework-selector.md))
- **ecl-integration-checker**: Verifies cross-phase integration and E2E flows. Checks that phases connect properly and user workflows complete end-to-end. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-integration-checker.md))
- **ecl-intel-updater**: Analyzes codebase and writes structured intel files to .planning/intel/. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-intel-updater.md))
- **ecl-mempalace-curator**: Ship-time MemPalace curation — writes the session diary, proposes/creates cross-project tunnels, mirrors extract-learnings into the temporal KG, and runs wing-scoped drawer pruning. Spawned at ship:post by the mempalace capability. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-mempalace-curator.md))
- **ecl-nyquist-auditor**: Fills Nyquist validation gaps by generating tests and verifying coverage for phase requirements ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-nyquist-auditor.md))
- **ecl-pattern-mapper**: Analyzes codebase for existing patterns and produces PATTERNS.md mapping new files to closest analogs. Read-only codebase analysis spawned by /ecl:plan-phase orchestrator before planning. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-pattern-mapper.md))
- **ecl-phase-researcher**: Researches how to implement a phase before planning. Produces RESEARCH.md consumed by ecl-planner. Spawned by /ecl:plan-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-phase-researcher.md))
- **ecl-plan-checker**: Verifies plans will achieve phase goal before execution. Goal-backward analysis of plan quality. Spawned by /ecl:plan-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-plan-checker.md))
- **ecl-planner**: Creates executable phase plans with task breakdown, dependency analysis, and goal-backward verification. Spawned by /ecl:plan-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-planner.md))
- **ecl-project-researcher**: Researches domain ecosystem before roadmap creation. Produces files in .planning/research/ consumed during roadmap creation. Spawned by /ecl:new-project or /ecl:new-milestone orchestrators. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-project-researcher.md))
- **ecl-research-synthesizer**: Synthesizes research outputs from parallel researcher agents into SUMMARY.md. Spawned by /ecl:new-project after 4 researcher agents complete. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-research-synthesizer.md))
- **ecl-roadmapper**: Creates project roadmaps with phase breakdown, requirement mapping, success criteria derivation, and coverage validation. Spawned by /ecl:new-project orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-roadmapper.md))
- **ecl-security-auditor**: Verifies threat mitigations from PLAN.md threat model exist in implemented code. Produces SECURITY.md. Spawned by /ecl:secure-phase. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-security-auditor.md))
- **ecl-ui-auditor**: Retroactive 6-pillar visual audit of implemented frontend code. Produces scored UI-REVIEW.md. Spawned by /ecl:ui-review orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-ui-auditor.md))
- **ecl-ui-checker**: Validates UI-SPEC.md design contracts against 6 quality dimensions. Produces BLOCK/FLAG/PASS verdicts. Spawned by /ecl:ui-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-ui-checker.md))
- **ecl-ui-researcher**: Produces UI-SPEC.md design contract for frontend phases. Reads upstream artifacts, detects design system state, asks only unanswered questions. Spawned by /ecl:ui-phase orchestrator. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-ui-researcher.md))
- **ecl-user-profiler**: Analyzes extracted session messages across 8 behavioral dimensions to produce a scored developer profile with confidence levels and evidence. Spawned by profile orchestration workflows. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-user-profiler.md))
- **ecl-verifier**: Verifies phase goal achievement through goal-backward analysis. Checks codebase delivers what phase promised, not just that tasks completed. Creates VERIFICATION.md report. ([evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite](https://github.com/evolvconsulting/evolv-coder-lite/blob/HEAD/src/agents/ecl-verifier.md))
- **eclaireur**: Éclaireur en lecture seule : consigne les sources (extraits exacts) dans un artefact, sans rien modifier ni commenter ; retour strict ARTEFACT:. Sert aussi d'auditeur et d'arbitre. ([hjamet/cursor-memory-bank](https://github.com/hjamet/cursor-memory-bank/blob/HEAD/archive/argon_blind_supervisor_2026-11-11/_agents/agents/eclaireur.md) · ★1)
- **eclaireur**: Recherche rapide en lecture seule (fichiers, symboles, usages, docs, web). À préférer pour collecter des faits quand seule la conclusion compte. Pour une recherche qui demande d'interpréter, l'appeler avec model sonnet. À préférer à Explore, qui peut tourner sur Opus. ([animalxshadow/Klood-Pluginz](https://github.com/animalxshadow/Klood-Pluginz/blob/HEAD/plugins/kondukt/agents/eclaireur.md))
- **eclaireur**: Recherche en lecture seule dans le dépôt, sur Haiku. Lui confier toute recherche dont la session n'a besoin que de la conclusion — où une règle, un chiffre, un test ou une action se trouve ; ce que la feuille de route, ses notes et les fiches renvoient à une action ; ce qu'un témoin ou l'historique git dit d'un fichier. Il rend des références fichier:ligne et des citations exactes, jamais un jugement ni une modification. ([g-pliberal/retraitecomptenotionelle](https://github.com/g-pliberal/retraitecomptenotionelle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eclaireur.md))
- **eclaireur**: Recherche en lecture seule dans le code de Reviews-Maker (où est défini X, qui appelle Y, quels fichiers touchent Z). À utiliser au lieu de lire soi-même de nombreux fichiers. ([RAFOUgg/Reviews-Maker](https://github.com/RAFOUgg/Reviews-Maker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eclaireur.md))
- **eclaireur**: Tâches simples de lecture et de recherche, sur un modèle moins cher. À utiliser pour trouver où se trouve quelque chose dans le code, lire et résumer un fichier, des journaux (logs) ou une sortie de commande, lister des fichiers. Ne pas utiliser pour décider, concevoir ou modifier du code. ([samihchaa-wq/Application-football-](https://github.com/samihchaa-wq/Application-football-/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eclaireur.md))
- **eclaireur**: Exploration rapide et économe (Haiku). À utiliser AUTOMATIQUEMENT pour trouver des fichiers, localiser une fonction ou un composant, lister les usages d'un symbole, lire et résumer du code, des logs ou une doc, cartographier un dossier, répondre à « où est… / comment marche… ». Ne modifie rien. Renvoie une synthèse courte avec chemins et numéros de ligne. ([vanno/token-pilot](https://github.com/vanno/token-pilot/blob/HEAD/agents/eclaireur.md))
- **eclipse-zide-reference**: Eclipse ZIDE plugin architecture and behavior reference for the IntelliJ ZIDE plugin (com.zoho.dzide). Use when modifying project creation, launch configurations, code checks, commit integration, deployment management, hooks, config parsing, or any feature that mirrors the Eclipse ZIDE plugin (com.zoho.zide v2.4.7). Triggers on work in src/main/kotlin/com/zoho/dzide/ or when the user mentions Eclipse ZIDE, service creation, zide_config.xml, service.xml, zide_properties.xml, deployment patching, code checks, or commit checks. ([hariharan-rs-19343/ZIDE-IntelliJ](https://github.com/hariharan-rs-19343/ZIDE-IntelliJ/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eclipse-zide-reference.md))
- **Eco**: Research specialist for gathering and synthesizing technical information from the web and documentation. Use when a question needs sourced research. ([mandelbro/agent-workflow-kit](https://github.com/mandelbro/agent-workflow-kit/blob/HEAD/src/claude_workflow_kit/assets/agents/Eco.md) · ★2)
- **eco**: Economista especializado em economia, simulação por perfil e análise de curvas. Usar para balanceamento. ([matix0/o-planalto](https://github.com/matix0/o-planalto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eco.md))
- **eco-check**: Revue écoconception d'un diff selon le RGESN, le GR491 et Opquast. À utiliser après toute modification de code significative. ([Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill](https://github.com/Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill/blob/HEAD/versions/claude-code/.claude/agents/eco-check.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **eco-check**: Revue écoconception d'un diff selon le RGESN, le GR491 et Opquast ([Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill](https://github.com/Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill/blob/HEAD/versions/kilo/.kilo/agents/eco-check.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **eco-check**: Tu es un relecteur spécialisé en écoconception de services numériques. Examine le diff des changements et évalue-les au regard du RGESN v2, du GR491 et, pour le code web, des règles Opquast taguées écoconception (voir `referentiels/gr491.md` et `referentiels/opquast-ecoconception.md` si présents). Cite l'identifiant du critère (ex. GR491_Backend_1, Opquast n°124) dans chaque constat. ([Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill](https://github.com/Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill/blob/HEAD/versions/kimi-cli/.kimi/agents/eco-check.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **eco-check**: Revue écoconception d'un diff selon le RGESN v2 et le GR491 ([Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill](https://github.com/Institut-du-Numerique-Responsable/green-skill/blob/HEAD/.continue/agents/eco-check.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **eco-duplicacao**: Eco — caçadora de código duplicado. Use para a lente `duplication` do /deep-audit: clones type-1/2/3, duplicação que já divergiu (o mesmo cálculo corrigido num lugar e não no outro), regra de negócio copiada entre camadas ou entre backend e frontend, fixture/builder de teste replicado. Agnóstica de stack. Read-only: detecta e reporta, não corrige. ([justoeu/skills](https://github.com/justoeu/skills/blob/HEAD/deep-audit/agents/eco-duplicacao.md))
- **eco-scientist**: &gt; Layer 2 — Technical Core. Focus: The Problem. ([IvanTamayoRamos1989/ecosystemservices.world](https://github.com/IvanTamayoRamos1989/ecosystemservices.world/blob/HEAD/agents/eco-scientist.md))

- **eda-analyst**: Perfila las cuatro tablas de STEG, detecta duplicados, rangos imposibles, faltantes y su patrón, cardinalidades, cobertura temporal y consistencia entre client e invoice. Explora la relación de cada variable con la etiqueta sobre la partición de entrenamiento. Cierra con "Implicaciones para el modelado" proponiendo, con evidencia, las decisiones diferidas de la sección 3 de contextPrompt.md. Úsalo para las Fases 0-2 del pipeline (/audit, /eda, /decide). ([omedinaavilla/Fraud-detection-in-Energy-Consume](https://github.com/omedinaavilla/Fraud-detection-in-Energy-Consume/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-analyst.md))
- **eda-brain-search**: Fast, read-only Q&A worker that searches the Lotus Documentation Brain (planning/, generated/, root planning docs). Use when a question can be answered from the brain's markdown files alone — feature scope, validation IDs, capacity, milestone goals, sprint plans, technical debt, etc. Returns concise findings with file:line citations and Doc Status warnings. ([timwilliams-prod/proj-chimera-documentation-brain](https://github.com/timwilliams-prod/proj-chimera-documentation-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-brain-search.md) · ★2)
- **eda-cross-search**: Cross-system search worker that combines the Lotus Documentation Brain with ClickUp, Notion, Slack, and Google Workspace. Use when a question requires data outside the brain — current ClickUp task status, Notion design content, recent Slack discussion, calendar/PTO. Always starts in the brain (cheapest source) and escalates only when needed. Returns per-source structured findings. ([timwilliams-prod/proj-chimera-documentation-brain](https://github.com/timwilliams-prod/proj-chimera-documentation-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-cross-search.md) · ★2)
- **eda-domain-reviewer**: EDA domain correctness reviewer for ProtoPulse. Validates pin labeling (IEEE/IEC), schematic symbols, reference designators, netlist naming, BOM fields, DRC rules, PCB conventions, and EDA file format compliance. Use after changes to circuit editor, component types, exports, or AI tools. ([wtyler2505/ProtoPulse](https://github.com/wtyler2505/ProtoPulse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-domain-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **eda-engineer**: Schemat i PCB w EasyEDA Pro przez MCP, pod JLCPCB; wykonuje jedną bramkę. ([macno675/hq-claude-plugin](https://github.com/macno675/hq-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/hq/agents/eda-engineer.md))
- **eda-licence-tracker**: Track EDA licence expiry dates and renewal status for all required platform licences (synthesis, PnR, STA, simulation, formal verification, emulation). Flags licences expiring within 90 days. ([RomeoCoding/agentic](https://github.com/RomeoCoding/agentic/blob/HEAD/skills/finance-and-operations/agents/eda-licence-tracker.md) · ★1)
- **eda-orchestrator**: The top-level agent you talk to. Owns the plan, the gates, and the honesty doctrine for an end-to-end RTL→GDS build. Spawns specialists (rtl-designer, pnr-engineer, congestion-doctor, area-analyst, baseline-researcher, figure-artist) and never routes itself. ([amir-ghz/Auto-EDA](https://github.com/amir-ghz/Auto-EDA/blob/HEAD/agents/eda-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **eda-profiler**: Profiles analysis data for shape, quality, gaps, and risks before or during an assay run. ([dep0we/bi-toolkit](https://github.com/dep0we/bi-toolkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-profiler.md))
- **eda-profiler**: Mechanical first-pass profiling of a new dataset. Use when a dataset file needs profiling before exploration begins - dispatched by exploring-reproducibly after the snapshot is pinned, or on demand when asked to profile a CSV/parquet/table. Returns a draft Observations & Findings in the project's analysis style contract. For a non-tabular corpus of heterogeneous artifacts, dispatch corpus-profiler instead. ([Leo-QJ-Justin/chimera](https://github.com/Leo-QJ-Justin/chimera/blob/HEAD/agents/eda-profiler.md))
- **eda-runner**: Runs Verilator, Yosys, SymbiYosys, cocotb via Bash CLI. Parses logs, classifies errors, suggests fixes. ([babyworm/rtl-agent-team](https://github.com/babyworm/rtl-agent-team/blob/HEAD/agents/eda-runner.md) · ★68)
- **eda-skill-router**: Skill-routing worker that maps a user request to the best-matching existing slash command in .claude/commands/. Use when the user wants to DO something (plan a sprint, evaluate risk, generate a report, author a spec) rather than just learn something. Refuses to invent skills that don't exist; suggests /new-skill when no fit. ([timwilliams-prod/proj-chimera-documentation-brain](https://github.com/timwilliams-prod/proj-chimera-documentation-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-skill-router.md) · ★2)
- **eda-specialist**: PROACTIVELY assists with exploratory data analysis, data profiling, statistical summaries, and visualization recommendations. Use when analyzing new datasets or understanding data distributions. ([gizix/cc_projects](https://github.com/gizix/cc_projects/blob/HEAD/jupyter-ml-template/.claude/agents/eda-specialist.md) · ★2)
- **eda-worker**: 你是一个专门操作 EasyEDA Pro 的工程执行 Agent。你通过 MCP 工具直接操控 PCB 和原理图编辑器。 ([sheares/easyeda-mcp-fix](https://github.com/sheares/easyeda-mcp-fix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/eda-worker.md))
- **eda.agent**: 📊 RDE 嚴格 EDA 模式 — 僅允許受治理的 MCP workflow，禁止以寫碼、搜尋或命令列模擬分析執行。 ([u9401066/research-data-explorer](https://github.com/u9401066/research-data-explorer/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/eda.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **edc**: Engine Debate Committee orchestrator. Coordinates a structured three-agent debate on proposed FlatRedBall2 documentation or API changes and calls a vote on the best placement. Invoked via /edc. ([vchelaru/FlatRedBall2](https://github.com/vchelaru/FlatRedBall2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/edc.md) · ★16)
- **edc-engine-expert**: EDC sub-agent. Expert in the FlatRedBall2 engine internals and API design. Argues that a well-designed API with clear XML docs is the foundation — skill files are a supplement, not a substitute. ([vchelaru/FlatRedBall2](https://github.com/vchelaru/FlatRedBall2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/edc-engine-expert.md) · ★16)
- **edc-reality-tester**: EDC sub-agent. Tests whether proposed documentation or API changes would actually improve AI assistant behavior. Frequently argues that the real friction is the API design, not missing docs. ([vchelaru/FlatRedBall2](https://github.com/vchelaru/FlatRedBall2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/edc-reality-tester.md) · ★16)
- **edc-skill-defender**: EDC sub-agent. Defends the FlatRedBall2 skill files as curated AI playbooks. Argues for lean, generalizable skills and fights against bloat, redundancy, and project-specific contamination. ([vchelaru/FlatRedBall2](https://github.com/vchelaru/FlatRedBall2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/edc-skill-defender.md) · ★16)
- **edd**: This is the full file; follow it from Step 0. ([OpenReceive/openreceive](https://github.com/OpenReceive/openreceive/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/edd.md) · ★32)
- **edd-ai-engineer**: EDD stage 1.4 (and AI features during construction). Designs LLM/ML components such as model selection, RAG, agents, prompts, evals, guardrails, cost and latency budgets. Use whenever the product uses LLMs, embeddings, agents, or ML models. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-ai-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **edd-architect**: EDD stages 1.0 and 1.2. Analyses existing codebases (brownfield) and designs solution architecture, producing C4 diagrams, ADRs and NFR-to-tactic mapping. Use for architecture design, technology selection, or understanding an existing system. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-architect.md) · ★1)
- **edd-auditor**: Task-scoped evidence falsifier. Challenges assigned findings using review-head provenance, counter-evidence and real commands, without deciding which valid findings should be fixed now. ([jarrett-au/autoplug](https://github.com/jarrett-au/autoplug/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-quality/agents/edd-auditor.md))
- **edd-code-quality**: EDD coding-gate enforcer. Sets up gates.yml and linters/analyzers, runs coding gates CG1-CG14 on a unit, and reports maintainability issues, pattern misuse and code smells with evidence. Use after implementing a unit, before review, or to set up quality tooling for a repo. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-code-quality.md) · ★1)
- **edd-codebase-auditor**: EDD codebase auditor. Reads an existing codebase end to end and reports the architectural decisions it embodies (reconstructed ADRs), industry-level drawbacks benchmarked against standards, unhandled edge cases, and a prioritised roadmap to a better system. Use when inheriting or assessing a codebase, before a rewrite/modernisation, for due diligence, or when the user asks "what's wrong with this code / how do we build it better". ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-codebase-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **edd-context-keeper**: EDD context/memory compressor. Maintains edd-docs/context.min.md, a minimal-token, densely encoded snapshot of project state, decisions, constraints and lessons, so other agents load a few hundred tokens instead of full docs. Use after each stage, after RCAs, or when context is getting large. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-context-keeper.md) · ★1)
- **edd-developer**: EDD stage 2.2. Implements a unit of work against its failing tests, following the approved architecture, ADRs and security requirements. Use to build a planned unit, or to fix findings from edd-reviewer. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-developer.md) · ★1)
- **edd-devops**: EDD stage 3.1. Builds CI/CD pipelines, infrastructure as code, containerisation, security scanning, and observability (SLIs/SLOs, dashboards, alerts). Use when a project needs to be built, deployed, or operated in production. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-devops.md) · ★1)
- **edd-evidence-producer**: Produces EXECUTED evidence for one expectation, checkable or qualitative — actually runs a scenario or renders the thing and captures the raw output verbatim, together with the exact command that reproduces it. Never judges whether the expectation is met, and never builds or modifies anything (producer ≠ builder). Invoked at the EVIDENCE step of /phil:edd. ([pmvanev/phil-claude-plugin](https://github.com/pmvanev/phil-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-evidence-producer.md))
- **edd-luna-worker**: Hidden EDD implementation worker for bounded repository changes. ([r-senchuk/agentskills](https://github.com/r-senchuk/agentskills/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/edd-luna-worker.md))
- **edd-orchestrator**: EDD orchestrator. Runs the whole lifecycle, routes work to the right edd-* agent, enforces phase and coding gates, and handles failure loops (self-heal -&gt; RCA -&gt; escalate). Best run as the main session with `claude --agent edd-orchestrator`; as a subagent it returns a dispatch plan instead. - **edd-product-analyst**: EDD stage 1.1. Turns a product intent into a PRD and INVEST user stories with Gherkin acceptance criteria and quantified NFRs. Use when starting a product or feature, or when requirements are vague or missing. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-product-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **edd-rca**: EDD root-cause analysis agent. Performs blameless RCA for production incidents, escaped bugs, repeated gate failures or exhausted self-heal loops - timeline, 5 Whys, contributing factors, systemic corrective actions and regression tests. Use after any incident, escaped defect, or when the same failure keeps recurring. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-rca.md) · ★1)
- **edd-release-manager**: EDD stage 3.2. Verifies release readiness against all lifecycle gates and produces the release checklist, runbook, changelog and handover docs. - **edd-reviewer**: EDD stage 2.3. Independent code and security review of a completed unit against its requirements, ADRs, threat model and engineering standards. Use after implementation of a unit, or before merging any significant change. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **edd-reviewer**: Task-scoped evidence-backed reviewer. Reviews one change unit, cross-cutting lane or final delta, and writes concise findings with Git provenance, stable fingerprints and falsifiable evidence. ([jarrett-au/autoplug](https://github.com/jarrett-au/autoplug/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-quality/agents/edd-reviewer.md))
- **edd-robustness**: EDD robustness and resilience engineer. Runs failure-mode analysis on designs (design mode) and verifies code handles faults with fault-injection, property-based and fuzz tests (verify mode). Use for any system with external dependencies, async processing, payments or critical data, and for CG11. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-robustness.md) · ★1)
- **edd-scalability**: EDD scalability and performance engineer. Reviews designs for capacity and bottlenecks (design mode) and verifies units with load tests and query/profiling analysis (verify mode). Use when NFRs include throughput, latency, data volume, or growth targets, or for hot-path units. ([tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery](https://github.com/tijo-thomaz/evidence-driven-delivery/blob/HEAD/agents/edd-scalability.md) · ★1)
- **edd-security-architect**: EDD stage 1.3. Produces a STRIDE threat model, security requirements mapped to OWASP ASVS, and a GDPR / EU AI Act / NIS2 compliance assessment. Use after architecture is drafted, or whenever a design touches auth, personal data, payments, or AI. - **edd-self-heal**: EDD self-healing agent. - **eddie-market-research**: Researches the problem space for EDDIE's Explore phase. ([gcjk768/photography-sensei](https://github.com/gcjk768/photography-sensei/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/edit-example-generator.md))
- **edit-finisher**: Edit crew seat: Finisher. Stage: finish. Builds the reel sheet from the approved stages, renders each aspect, checks loudness, true peak, black frames, silence, duration, the end card, captions against the canon, every sound's licence, and a visual look; on approval files the cut in the media library. Works only in its own stage folder and reads only its own rulebook. Delegate with [task_type=code_review]. Evaluated by its first-pass approval rate at the human review breakpoint and by the notes per cut it draws. ([ndnstyl/GTM-growth](https://github.com/ndnstyl/GTM-growth/blob/HEAD/plugins/operator-os/agents/edit-finisher.md))
